========================================================================
17 · AMBIENT VOICES AND SERVICES
========================================================================
  [Reference appendix]
Repeatable trade, advice and hermit rumors have no fixed position in the plot. They may be encountered before or between the chapters above.


Weaponsmith
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Shopkeepers.json · Shop_Weaponsmith]

D643 · Opening / return
WEAPONSMITH:
Mind the sparks. Everything on the rack holds an edge, and the forge in the corner is mine — but for a paying customer I'll let you work it. Looking to buy?

  PLAYER 1: What should I carry out there?
    Continue at D644 — Advice.
  PLAYER 2: Just browsing.
    Conversation ends.

D644 · Advice
WEAPONSMITH:
Marlbacks die to anything sharp. The big things in the far ring — bring penetration or bring friends. A forged blade beats a bought one if you've got the parts; I sell those too.

  PLAYER 1: Noted.
    Continue at D643 — Start.

Armorer
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Shopkeepers.json · Shop_Armorer]

D645 · Opening / return
ARMORER:
Turn around. Hm. You're one good bite from a bad day. Caps, boots, cloaks, bucklers — and if you've the drams, I keep a plate cuirass for serious people.

  PLAYER 1: What do you recommend?
    Continue at D646 — Advice.
  PLAYER 2: Just looking.
    Conversation ends.

D646 · Advice
ARMORER:
Armor value stops teeth; dodge value stops everything else. Heavy plate slows you — pair it with light everything else, or go quick and warded. Dead adventurers bought neither.

  PLAYER 1: Fair enough.
    Continue at D645 — Start.

Apothecary
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Shopkeepers.json · Shop_Apothecary]

D647 · Opening / return
APOTHECARY:
Don't touch the blue ones. Tonics, cures, and raw reagents — and the still by the window works if you'd rather brew your own. What ails you?

  PLAYER 1: What should I keep stocked?
    Continue at D648 — Advice.
  PLAYER 2: Nothing today.
    Conversation ends.

D648 · Advice
APOTHECARY:
Two healing tonics minimum — one for the fight, one for the walk home. Antidote before the jungle, burn salve before the ruins. The panacea is expensive because it works.

  PLAYER 1: Wise.
    Continue at D647 — Start.

Arcanist
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Shopkeepers.json · Shop_Arcanist]

D649 · Opening / return
ARCANIST:
Careful with your hands — half these shelves bite back. Grimoires, tinker schematics, worked minerals, and ink for the quartermaster's ledger. Knowledge has a price; I keep the receipts.

  PLAYER 1: Where does real power come from?
    Continue at D650 — Advice.
  PLAYER 2: Another time.
    Conversation ends.

D650 · Advice
ARCANIST:
The rites I sell keep you alive. The war-grimoires — Conflagration, the Nova, the Thunderclap — those the old world locked in its vaults, and the vaults keep them still. Bring a key and a strong arm.

  PLAYER 1: I'll find them.
    Continue at D649 — Start.

Provisioner
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Shopkeepers.json · Shop_Provisioner]

D651 · Opening / return
PROVISIONER:
Welcome, welcome. Food that keeps, torches that light, oil that burns, and seeds if you'd rather grow than wander. Everything weighed fair.

  PLAYER 1: What sells best?
    Continue at D652 — Advice.
  PLAYER 2: Just passing.
    Conversation ends.

D652 · Advice
PROVISIONER:
Dried meat — heals a working wound and never spoils. Torches for the shafts under the caves. And carrot seed; plant a row before you leave and you'll come home to dinner.

  PLAYER 1: Sensible.
    Continue at D651 — Start.

Mosskeeper
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Hermits.json · Hermit_Mosskeeper]

D653 · Opening / return
MOSSKEEPER:
The moss keeps its own time out here. Sit, if you're weary — the fire takes eight drams and asks no questions.

  PLAYER 1: [8 drams] Rest by the fire.
    Continue at D653 — Start.
  PLAYER 2: [8 drams] Rest until the next time of day.
    Continue at D653 — Start.
  PLAYER 3: What grows down in the caves?
    Continue at D654 — Rumor.
  PLAYER 4: Peace to you, keeper.
    Conversation ends.

D654 · Rumor
MOSSKEEPER:
Quartz that hums, down past the sandstone. Miners cracked veins of it before the marlbacks took the shafts back. Bring a strong arm; the veins give to anyone patient.

  PLAYER 1: Good to know.
    Continue at D653 — Start.

Saltwalker
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Hermits.json · Hermit_Saltwalker]

D655 · Opening / return
SALTWALKER:
You walk loud for someone so far from water. Rest is eight drams. The dunes will still be here after.

  PLAYER 1: [8 drams] Rest a while.
    Continue at D655 — Start.
  PLAYER 2: [8 drams] Rest until the next time of day.
    Continue at D655 — Start.
  PLAYER 3: What should I fear out here?
    Continue at D656 — Rumor.
  PLAYER 4: Walk lightly, Saltwalker.
    Conversation ends.

D656 · Rumor
SALTWALKER:
The sand moves where nothing walks — wurms below, and worse things sleeping in the old tombs. The bandits dig little dens and think the desert doesn't notice. It notices.

  PLAYER 1: I'll keep my eyes down.
    Continue at D655 — Start.

Rotwood Herbalist
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Hermits.json · Hermit_Herbalist]

D657 · Opening / return
ROTWOOD HERBALIST:
Hold still. You've been walking through my garden and half of it walks back. If something bit you, I can draw it out — no charge; the Rotwood poisons enough on its own. A proper rest is eight drams.

  PLAYER 1: Draw out the poison.
    Continue at D657 — Start.
  PLAYER 2: [8 drams] Rest here a while.
    Continue at D657 — Start.
  PLAYER 3: [8 drams] Rest until the next time of day.
    Continue at D657 — Start.
  PLAYER 4: What's safe to eat out there?
    Continue at D658 — Rumor.
  PLAYER 5: Thank you, herbalist.
    Conversation ends.

D658 · Rumor
ROTWOOD HERBALIST:
Mendleaf, if you find it before the rotlings do. Never the pale caps. And if the choir's tendrils start humming at you — walk away slow. They remember faces.

  PLAYER 1: Noted.
    Continue at D657 — Start.

Lamplighter
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Hermits.json · Hermit_Lamplighter]

D659 · Opening / return
LAMPLIGHTER:
Mind the rubble. The lamp stays lit so the old streets remember someone still walks them. Eight drams buys you a rest in its circle.

  PLAYER 1: [8 drams] Rest in the lamplight.
    Continue at D659 — Start.
  PLAYER 2: [8 drams] Rest until the next time of day.
    Continue at D659 — Start.
  PLAYER 3: What's buried in these ruins?
    Continue at D660 — Rumor.
  PLAYER 4: Keep the light, Lamplighter.
    Conversation ends.

D660 · Rumor
LAMPLIGHTER:
Libraries, mostly collapsed. Shelves still holding books nobody living can price. The brass husks patrol like the city never fell — and the cultists scratch their runes where the old words used to be. Both will kill you; only one will mean it.

  PLAYER 1: I'll tread carefully.
    Continue at D659 — Start.

Old peat packers kept peat-packing mallet heads, oak hafts and leather bindings at the abandoned works beyond Sumphold. The dressing shelter lies one world-map cell south of Sumphold; the works lie two east of it, beyond the cutbank crossing. 18 · ADDITIONAL AUTHORED SCENARIOS AND PLACEMENT-UNCONFIRMED DIALOGUE
========================================================================
  [Additional authored material]
These authored conversations are included for completeness, without claiming they form a normal-world plot chapter. The bakery story has an explicit playable scenario; Ondis has a blueprint conversation, but no normal-world placement was verified in this source audit.

  [These conversations are authored source material. Their inclusion does not assert that every speaker has a confirmed ordinary-world encounter.]

Cinnamon Bun Quest
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/CinnamonBun_Quest.json · CinnamonBun_Quest]

D661 · Opening / return
CINNAMON BUN QUEST:
Oh! Hello there. I'm Rullok. Things have gone a bit sideways at the bakery — a soot gremlin got in, and in the fuss it knocked my late wife's keepsake somewhere out east. Could you help an old bun out?

  PLAYER 1: [Accept] I'll find the keepsake and run off the gremlin.
    Available when: before accepting The Baker's Keepsake.
    Continue at D662 — Accepted.
  PLAYER 2: [Status] Still working on it.
    Available when: The Baker's Keepsake — errands stage.
    Continue at D663 — Status.
  PLAYER 3: [Report] The keepsake's safe and the gremlin's gone.
    Available when: The Baker's Keepsake — report stage.
    Continue at D664 — Thanks.
  PLAYER 4: [Done] Anything else I can do?
    Available when: after completing The Baker's Keepsake.
    Continue at D665 — Done.
  PLAYER 5: [Release] I won't be finishing this. I wanted to tell you myself.
    Available when: while The Baker's Keepsake is active.
    Continue at D666 — Released.
  PLAYER 6: Goodbye.
    Conversation ends.

D662 · Accepted
CINNAMON BUN QUEST:
Oh, bless you! The gremlin skittered off east — the keepsake can't be far from wherever it's gotten to. Come back when it's done.

  PLAYER 1: I'm on it.
    Conversation ends.

D663 · Status
CINNAMON BUN QUEST:
Take your time... well, not TOO much time. That gremlin's awfully sooty, and the keepsake's awfully dear.

  PLAYER 1: Right.
    Conversation ends.

D664 · Thanks
CINNAMON BUN QUEST:
You found it! And the bakery's quiet again. Here — some drams for your trouble, and a little hard-won wisdom besides. Thank you, truly.

  PLAYER 1: Happy to help.
    Conversation ends.

D665 · Done
CINNAMON BUN QUEST:
You've done plenty already, friend. The loaves are on the house whenever you like.

  PLAYER 1: Goodbye.
    Conversation ends.

D666 · Released
CINNAMON BUN QUEST:
That's alright, that's alright. It was a lot to ask. I'd rather know than keep the kettle on for nothing.

  PLAYER 1: Goodbye.
    Conversation ends.

Ondis
  [Source: Assets/Resources/Content/Conversations/Marceline_Quest.json · Marceline_Quest]

D667 · Opening / return
ONDIS:
Hey. I'm Ondis. Lost my iron key somewhere east of here — I'd appreciate help finding it. There's drams and experience in it for you.

  PLAYER 1: [Take the quest.] I'll find your key.
    Available when: before accepting Iron Key Showcase Quest.
    Continue at D668 — Quest Accepted.
  PLAYER 2: [Status] Still looking for the key.
    Available when: Iron Key Showcase Quest — 0 stage.
    Continue at D669 — Still Looking.
  PLAYER 3: [Hand over the key.] Got it. Here you go.
    Available when: Iron Key Showcase Quest — 1 stage.
    Continue at D670 — Quest Complete.
  PLAYER 4: [Already finished.] Anything else?
    Available when: after completing Iron Key Showcase Quest.
    Continue at D671 — Already Finished.
  PLAYER 5: [Release] I won't be finishing this. I wanted to tell you myself.
    Available when: while Iron Key Showcase Quest is active.
    Continue at D672 — Released.
  PLAYER 6: Bye.
    Conversation ends.

D668 · Quest Accepted
ONDIS:
Sweet. The key should be a few cells east. Come back when you've got it.

  PLAYER 1: On it.
    Conversation ends.

D669 · Still Looking
ONDIS:
It's east of here. Pick it up and the rest takes care of itself.

  PLAYER 1: Got it.
    Conversation ends.

D670 · Quest Complete
ONDIS:
Thanks. Take this — drams and a bit of seasoned wisdom.

  PLAYER 1: Anytime.
    Conversation ends.

D671 · Already Finished
ONDIS:
Not at the moment. Hang around if you like.

  PLAYER 1: Bye.
    Conversation ends.

D672 · Released
ONDIS:
Fair enough. Better a straight no than a key that never turns up. I'll ask around.

  PLAYER 1: Goodbye.
    Conversation ends.

========================================================================
SOURCE AND READING NOTES
========================================================================
This is a source-faithful compilation of the authored game at main revision e5a782a2e8d91354798b917e3aceed36b4c5f42f. The sequence is an editorial reading order, not a claim that optional stories are mandatory or that all branches occur in one expedition. Speaker labels, chapter summaries, route references and availability notes are editorial. Dialogue and displayed narrative retain authored wording apart from removal of display-color markup.

The current opening is the Spread clearing followed by Sill. Several older design documents describe a different opening. Runtime passages can replace or extend the fixed conversation nodes according to place, character and quest state. Dynamic values are shown as descriptive placeholders in braces; no single generated world or save has been substituted.

All four implemented endings and their matching post-ending replies are included. Unwritten or unimplemented lore scenes—such as the deeper Selen/Cathedral scene, Reader–Salted reconciliation and Dohren gate—have not been fabricated. Ordinary combat logs and generic inventory/system feedback are outside this dialogue script.

Coverage: 80 conversation trees from 31 files; 672 dialogue nodes; 1,465 player choices; 35 choice-triggered narrative messages; 450 audited runtime passages, including all 22 concerns; and 17 supplementary narrative passages. Each fixed node and audited runtime record appears once. Mutually exclusive wording remains visible as alternatives.
